### Run 313 (sal_emu300, mode 'tr313') — refresh control
### flow traced: the execute returns; extraction dies
### before the store chain

Same ladder; PC hooks inside FUN_61949679 during the
refresh phase. Hits, in order:
  61949679 refresh-entry            FIRED
  619497be call FUN_619013e7        FIRED (so the SAL
        execute RETURNED to native code)
  619497f8 call FUN_6190141f #1     never
  61949800 call FUN_6190141f #2     never
  6194980b call FUN_619013cb #2     never
  619499fb call FUN_61947d71        never
  6190141f entry (any, refr phase)  never
sel3's execute GET trace (native drive): module[17],
method[3], GET(record,1) = 65535 — three GETs and out,
identical to stream-level run 311. The method reads the
unbound type field and produces an EMPTY reply.
Refresh then dies in the extraction prologue — at/inside
the FUN_619013e7 reply-status read (or the immediately
following FUN_619013cb / FUN_61952248) — and unwinds
through its SEH frame; the doc-store chain is never
reached (consistent with run 312's zero doc-writes).
CONCLUSION: refresh is a CONSUMER of a bound doc, not
the writer. Its store copies sel3's reply string into
ctx+0x24; sel3 returns a string only when it has one,
and with [1] = -1 it returns nothing, which the native
extraction treats as fatal. The binding step is NOT in
the PCMTOATP family at all (init mints unbound; ckey /
sel4 / sel3 read or guard on the doc; forwarders and
the getter don't touch it). Run 314 pinpoints the exact
extraction death call for the record.

### Run 314 (sal_emu301, mode 'tr314') — death point
### pinpointed: refresh throws sel3's error via
### FUN_61952248, the family error-thrower

Refined PC hooks, same ladder. Refresh-phase hits:
  61949679 entry -> 619497be call 013e7 -> 619013e7
  thunk -> 619497c3 (013e7 RETURNED, reply-status byte
  consumed) -> 619497c9 call 013cb#1 (RETURNED, error
  value extracted into the -0x48 pair) -> 619497d5 call
  FUN_61952248 -> NEVER RETURNS. No 0141f call, no
  61947d71 call.
FUN_61952248 (1228 B, decompiled): an ostringstream-
based error formatter/thrower — builds "OmgConv2: ..."
messages, logs codes > 99 via procfile vt+0x58, and
throws OMGExceptionImpl for a non-zero code (case 0 is
the only clean return). So sel3's reply DOES carry an
element natively (invisible to run 311's readback
probe): an error status, which 013e7/013cb extract and
refresh hands to the thrower. Refresh's unwind through
its SEH frame (the 0x61949a69 stop EAX of runs 312-314)
is that throw propagating. Refresh is error-checked
plumbing around sel3, full stop.

### TASK 1 — sel0 stream grammar RE-DERIVED (byte-exact)
From FUN_61948a93's decomp (out/decomp-s46-omgconv2.c,

in every ladder since S48):

[ebp+0x10]=1 DISCRIMINANT, [ebp+0x14]=0 FAMILY MODE
(-> ctx+0x20, range-checked 0..2 else E_INVALIDARG;
NOT a stream element).
Stream (bind descriptor "nc.ocm" via FUN_619015fc /
FUN_61901383; execute mgr vt+0):
 [0] sessrec — FUN_6190139c: mgr vt+0x8() session
     record appended {ptr,16} via vt+0x6c; bytes
     01 0f 50 00 00 04 00 00 00 00 00 16 72 5f 9b e7.
 [1] int64 — FUN_61901334(lo=[ebp+0x10], hi=sign-ext):
     the DISCRIMINANT (canonical 1; S51: stream 0,1 run

     (canonical 12 = ATRAC3plus 64k; At3::getParam

     then method GET [selector], S53 run 312 trace).
     Constant in all omgconv2 streams; the kdrmnc
     family (FUN_61939aef) omits it.
So Thread A's matrix = payload [sessrec][disc][mode]
dispatched at selector X, reading back whatever each

 ea50: [blob][int][blob][blob][blob][blob][str][str]

 f770: [blob][2][24]
 osss b230: [int 2] only, RD-BLOB; b510: [str][str][str]

 streaming: bad session-ID" string lives here).
KEY DIVERGENCES from omgconv2: (1) the trailing pair's
last int is 24/25/26/27 (four groups), NOT a constant
17 — with the module NAME constant "nc.ocm", the last
int cannot be a per-process module index of that one
program; it is either a program-slot inside the .ocm
file (nc.ocm exporting several programs; PCMTOATP = the
slot the harness knows as 17) or a session-specific
index — runtime must arbitrate (run 317 probes module

### TASK 3 — salwrap export census (desk, re-verified
### this session with pefile on the wineprefix copy)
salwrap.dll exports EXACTLY three functions:
salwrap0 (ord 1, 0x65103940), salwrap1 (ord 2,
0x65116670), salwrap2 (ord 3, 0x6510e1e0) — the manager
factory/entry points every host already uses. There
is NO exported doc-bind, value-mutating, or
type-registering API a host application could call
outside a SAL program execution. Binding can therefore
only happen (i) inside a SAL program executed through
the manager, in a session whose loaded-program set
differs from the ceremony reproduced here, or (ii) in
a host-side COM layer above the broker whose session
setup this harness has not driven (omgatpcd.dll's own
session management above PCMTOATP).

### TASK 4 DESK (done before run 319 landed) — omgatpcd.dll
### has NO session layer above PCMTOATP; the brief's
### layering premise is corrected
Full read of out/decomp-omgatpcd.c (1,135 lines, the
whole DLL): exactly THREE worker functions —
FUN_616054cd (key-blob), FUN_6160571e (BIND_CONTENT),
FUN_61604e96 (media-info) — plus stream-builder helpers.
The ONLY module name in the DLL is "atpcd.ocm" (x3, one
per worker). There is NO reference to omgconv2,
PCMTOATP, CoCreateInstance, or any other .ocm; no
session creation, no broker creation path, nothing
before init — omgatpcd is a SIBLING leaf library of
omgconv2 (repository/bind side), not a layer above it.
Its workers were already driven natively in S40/S44.
The application layer that in a real burn calls BOTH
libraries is the SonicStage app itself (Omgjbox.exe —
NOT in the held corpus; repo-mirror holds only
OmgStartup.exe / omginit.exe as exes). Task 4's drive
half is therefore void as framed; what remains testable
is whether any step in omgatpcd's workers loads another
program — desk answer: no (atpcd.ocm only).

### Run 320 attempt 1 (drvA, sal_emu306) — INVALIDATED by
### my table finder (own goal #3); killed mid-run, log
### preserved as run320-invalid-nodrives.log
The most-frequent-vec fix tied 1-1 at dump time (dummy's
transient vec 0xc40febc0 vs control's 0xc40fe740) and
picked the stale one again -> count=0 -> the drive loop
read an empty element list and SKIPPED every drive.
Root cause understood: the per-execute dispatch wrapper
values are transient (descriptor slab recycled after
the execute), so the table must be read DURING dispatch.
sal_emu307: the GET callback itself performs the table
dump live when it sees the (17 -> 0) dispatch pair
(count sanity 1..64), and _tabdump55 trusts the live
dump. Rerun as run 320 (sal_emu307).

THE REMAINING FIELD, named precisely: (i) the host
registration path itself and whatever the SonicStage
APPLICATION (Omgjbox.exe — NOT in the held corpus; the
mirror holds only OmgStartup.exe/omginit.exe) does when
it assembles a real burn: registering the conversion
modules and driving PCMTOATP inside a session whose
state (repository, EKB, device/disc context) our
reproduced ceremony only approximates; (ii) modules
the host loads only when a real device/disc write
begins (the to-OMG converter family and osss.ocm are
the named candidates — they exist on disk, parse when
referenced, and can only bind if the host slots them);
(iii) first-run/registration state on a real install.
The Toshiba gold-standard capture (a real session's doc
token + a real doc's [1]) arbitrates all three.
Whether nc.ocm itself ciphers remains OPEN — no output
bytes exist; only a bound doc settles it. XOROP count
for the whole session: 0. No new Sony dev-trace strings
found in S55.
